 51+  6695                      ; Контролируем флаг обновления UIP (битовая маска #80 в регистре #0A)
 52+  6695 3E 0A                LD      A, #0A
 53+  6697 CD D5 66             CALL    read_byte_raw
 54+  669A 17                   RLA
 55+  669B 38 F8                JR      C, .wait_rtc
 56+  669D
 57+  669D                      ; Вычитываем BCD данные с атомарным сохранением в текстовые буферы
 58+  669D                      ; 1. Секунды (Ячейка #00)
 59+  669D 3E 00                LD      A, #00

 61+  66A2 CD FA 66             CALL    split_bcd
 62+  66A5 7C                   LD      A, H
 62+  66A6 32 63 66       LD (txt_sec+0), A
 63+  66A9 7D                   LD      A, L
 63+  66AA 32 64 66       LD (txt_sec+1), A
 64+  66AD

 69+  66B6 32 61 66       LD (txt_min+0), A
 70+  66B9 7D                   LD      A, L
 70+  66BA 32 62 66       LD (txt_min+1), A
 71+  66BD
 72+  66BD                      ; 3. Часы (Ячейка #04)
 73+  66BD 3E 04                LD      A, #04

 76+  66C6 32 5F 66       LD (txt_hour+0), A
 77+  66C9 7D                   LD      A, L
 77+  66CA 32 60 66       LD (txt_hour+1), A
 78+  66CD
 79+  66CD CD F3 66             CALL    close_cmos

 81+  66D1 E1                   POP     HL
 81+  66D2 C1             POP BC
 81+  66D3 F1             POP AF
 82+  66D4 C9                   RET
 83+  66D5
 84+  66D5              ; --- Атомарное низкоуровневое чтение байта из К512ВИ1 ---
 85+  66D5              read_byte_raw
 86+  66D5 01 F7 DF             LD      BC, #DFF7
 87+  66D8 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
 88+  66DA 01 F7 BF             LD      BC, #BFF7
 89+  66DD ED 78                IN      A, (C)
 90+  66DF C9                   RET
 91+  66E0
 92+  66E0              ; --- Атомарная низкоуровневая запись байта в К512ВИ1 ---
 93+  66E0              write_byte_raw
 94+  66E0 01 F7 DF             LD      BC, #DFF7
 95+  66E3 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
 96+  66E5 01 F7 BF             LD      BC, #BFF7
 97+  66E8 ED 69                OUT     (C), L
 98+  66EA C9                   RET
 99+  66EB
100+  66EB              open_cmos

102+  66EE 3E 80                LD      A, #80
103+  66F0 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
104+  66F2 C9                   RET
105+  66F3
106+  66F3              close_cmos
107+  66F3 01 F7 EF             LD      BC, #EFF7
108+  66F6 AF                   XOR     A
109+  66F7 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
110+  66F9 C9                   RET
111+  66FA
112+  66FA              ; --- Распаковка BCD полубайтов в ASCII символы ---
113+  66FA              split_bcd

115+  66FB E6 F0                AND     %11110000
116+  66FD 0F                   RRCA
116+  66FE 0F             RRCA
116+  66FF 0F             RRCA
116+  6700 0F             RRCA

118+  6703 67                   LD      H, A
119+  6704 F1                   POP     AF
120+  6705 E6 0F                AND     %00001111
121+  6707 C6 30                ADD     A, 48
122+  6709 6F                   LD      L, A
123+  670A C9                   RET

 11+  670B              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  670B              ; Open the single NeoGS package on TR-DOS and read its first sector to #C000.
 13+  670B              ; The same 256-byte buffer is reused for every following sector while the
 14+  670B              ; firmware is streamed to NeoGS. The complete file is NEVER staged in host RAM.
 15+  670B              ; OUT: Carry clear, first sector at #C000, file length and next-sector cursor set.
 16+  670B              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  670B              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  670B
 18+  670B                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  670B 21 C0 6A             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  670E 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
 21+  6711 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
 22+  6714 ED B0                ldir
 23+  6716 3E 43                ld      a, 'C'
 24+  6718 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  671B 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  671D 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  6720 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
 28+  6722 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 29+  6725 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  6726 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  6728 CA 8D 67             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  672B
 33+  672B                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  672B F5                   push    af
 35+  672C AF                   xor     a
 36+  672D 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  6730 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  6733 F1                   pop     af
 39+  6734 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6736 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  6739 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  673C 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  673D 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  6740 B0                   or      b
 45+  6741 C2 91 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  6744
 47+  6744                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  6744 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  6747 22 D0 6A             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  674A 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  674B B5                   or      l
 52+  674C CA 95 67             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  674F 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  6750 B7                   or      a
 55+  6751 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  6753 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  6754 FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6756 DA 95 67             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  6759              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  6759                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 61+  6759 2A D0 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 62+  675C 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 63+  675F 19                   add     hl, de
 64+  6760 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 65+  6763 BC                   cp      h
 66+  6764 C2 91 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 67+  6767 B7                   or      a
 68+  6768 CA 91 67             jp      z, .bad_descriptor
 69+  676B 32 CA 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 70+  676E
 71+  676E 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 72+  6771 FE 10                cp      16
 73+  6773 D2 91 67             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 74+  6776 32 CE 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 75+  6779 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 76+  677C 32 CD 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 77+  677F 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 78+  6782 22 C8 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 79+  6785
 80+  6785                      ; Read only the first sector now. Later sectors are fetched on demand
 81+  6785                      ; by upload_firmware after the preceding buffer bytes have been sent.
 82+  6785 CD A0 67             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
 83+  6788 DA 99 67             jp      c, .sector_read_failed
 84+  678B AF                   xor     a
 85+  678C C9                   ret
 86+  678D
 87+  678D              .file_not_found:
 88+  678D 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 89+  678F 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
 90+  6791              .bad_descriptor:
 91+  6791 3E 02                ld      a, 2
 92+  6793 18 06                jr      .load_failed
 93+  6795              .bad_length:
 94+  6795 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 95+  6797 18 02                jr      .load_failed
 96+  6799              .sector_read_failed:
 97+  6799 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 98+  679B              .load_failed:
 99+  679B 32 CF 6A             ld      (neo_fw_file_error), a
100+  679E 37                   scf
101+  679F C9                   ret
102+  67A0
103+  67A0              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
104+  67A0              ; Read one TR-DOS sector into the reusable #C000 buffer.
105+  67A0              ; Uses neo_fw_load_track/sector/remaining. Advances the cursor only after a
106+  67A0              ; successful read. OUT: carry clear; carry set on disk error/exhaustion.
107+  67A0              ; Clobbers AF, BC, DE, HL.
108+  67A0              neo_fw_read_next_sector:
109+  67A0 3A CA 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
110+  67A3 B7                   or      a
111+  67A4 20 02                jr      nz, .sector_available
112+  67A6 37                   scf
113+  67A7 C9                   ret
114+  67A8              .sector_available:
115+  67A8 3A CD 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
116+  67AB 57                   ld      d, a
117+  67AC 3A CE 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
118+  67AF 5F                   ld      e, a
119+  67B0 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
120+  67B3 06 01                ld      b, 1
121+  67B5 0E 05                ld      c, 5
122+  67B7 AF                   xor     a
123+  67B8 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
124+  67BB 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
125+  67BE CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
126+  67C1 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
127+  67C4 47                   ld      b, a
128+  67C5 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
129+  67C8 B0                   or      b
130+  67C9 20 21                jr      nz, .sector_io_error
131+  67CB
132+  67CB                      ; Advance TR-DOS cursor: sectors 0..15, then next track.
133+  67CB 3A CE 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
134+  67CE 3C                   inc     a
135+  67CF FE 10                cp      16
136+  67D1 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
137+  67D3 AF                   xor     a
138+  67D4 32 CE 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
139+  67D7 3A CD 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
140+  67DA 3C                   inc     a
141+  67DB 32 CD 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
142+  67DE 18 03                jr      .sector_cursor_advanced
143+  67E0              .sector_same_track:
144+  67E0 32 CE 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
145+  67E3              .sector_cursor_advanced:
146+  67E3 3A CA 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
147+  67E6 3D                   dec     a
148+  67E7 32 CA 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
149+  67EA B7                   or      a
150+  67EB C9                   ret
151+  67EC              .sector_io_error:
152+  67EC 37                   scf
153+  67ED C9                   ret
154+  67EE
155+  67EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
156+  67EE              ; upload_firmware
157+  67EE              ; IN : HL = #C000 reusable first-sector buffer, DE = total package length.
158+  67EE              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
159+  67EE              ;
160+  67EE              ; Cold/warm reset path:
161+  67EE              ;   #F4 cold restart
162+  67EE              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
163+  67EE              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
164+  67EE              ;   #13 jump to $4000
165+  67EE              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
166+  67EE              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
167+  67EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
168+  67EE              upload_firmware:
168+  67EE
169+  67EE                      ; Only the first sector is resident at #C000. Remaining sectors are
170+  67EE                      ; read into this same buffer as the firmware stream consumes them.
171+  67EE                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
172+  67EE 22 D2 6A             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
173+  67F1 ED 53 D4 6A          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
174+  67F5
175+  67F5                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
176+  67F5 7A                   ld      a, d
177+  67F6 B7                   or      a
178+  67F7 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
179+  67F9 7B                   ld      a, e
180+  67FA FE 03                cp      3
181+  67FC DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
182+  67FF              .package_header_present:
183+  67FF 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
184+  6800 32 D8 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
185+  6803 23                   inc     hl
186+  6804 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
187+  6805 32 D9 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
188+  6808 2A D8 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
189+  680B 7C                   ld      a, h
190+  680C B5                   or      l
191+  680D CA 9E 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
192+  6810                      ; Bootstrap must fit entirely in the first 256-byte sector because it
193+  6810                      ; is needed before the receiver can be started. The package itself may
194+  6810                      ; be much larger; only this first sector is buffered at a time.
195+  6810 7C                   ld      a, h
196+  6811 B7                   or      a
197+  6812 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
198+  6815 7D                   ld      a, l
199+  6816 FE FF                cp      255                   ; maximum length 254 bytes
200+  6818 D2 9E 69             jp      nc, .upload_failed
201+  681B
202+  681B                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
203+  681B 2A D4 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
204+  681E 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
205+  6821 B7                   or      a
206+  6822 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
207+  6824 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
208+  6827 ED 5B D8 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
209+  682B B7                   or      a
210+  682C ED 52                sbc     hl, de
211+  682E DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
212+  6831 22 DC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
213+  6834
214+  6834                      ; Bootstrap and the beginning of firmware follow the two-byte header
215+  6834                      ; in the first sector. The firmware pointer is advanced as bytes go.
216+  6834 2A D2 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
217+  6837 23                   inc     hl
218+  6838 23                   inc     hl
219+  6839 22 D6 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
220+  683C ED 5B D8 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
221+  6840 19                   add     hl, de
222+  6841 22 DA 6A             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
223+  6844
224+  6844                      ; Number of firmware bytes remaining in the first sector:
225+  6844                      ; 256 - (2-byte header + bootstrap length) = 254 - bootstrap length.
226+  6844 21 FE 00             ld      hl, 254
227+  6847 B7                   or      a
228+  6848 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
229+  684A 22 CB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
230+  684D
231+  684D                      ; Firmware image must fit below $3400, preserving at least 3 KiB for the stack below SP=$3FFF.
232+  684D 2A DC 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
233+  6850 7C                   ld      a, h
234+  6851 B7                   or      a
235+  6852 C2 5C 68             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
236+  6855 7D                   ld      a, l
237+  6856 B7                   or      a
238+  6857 CA 9E 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
239+  685A 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
240+  685C              .firmware_high_nonzero:
241+  685C 7C                   ld      a, h
242+  685D FE 34                cp      #34
243+  685F 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
244+  6861 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
245+  6864 7D                   ld      a, l
246+  6865 B7                   or      a
247+  6866 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
248+  6869              .firmware_length_valid:
249+  6869 AF                   xor     a
250+  686A 32 E8 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
251+  686D 32 E9 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
252+  6870 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
253+  6873 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
254+  6875 32 EA 6A             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
255+  6878 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
256+  687B
257+  687B                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
258+  687B                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
259+  687B 3E 01                ld      a, 1
260+  687D 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
261+  6880 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
262+  6882 CD CE 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
263+  6885 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
264+  6888
265+  6888                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
266+  6888                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
267+  6888                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
268+  6888                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
269+  6888 3E 02                ld      a, 2
270+  688A 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
271+  688D CD A0 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
272+  6890 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
273+  6892 3E 01                ld      a, 1
274+  6894 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
275+  6897 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
276+  6899 CD CE 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
277+  689C DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
278+  689F 3E 02                ld      a, 2
279+  68A1 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
280+  68A4 CD A0 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
281+  68A7 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
282+  68AA              .stock_rom_ready:
283+  68AA
284+  68AA                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
285+  68AA                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
286+  68AA                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
287+  68AA                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
288+  68AA 3E 03                ld      a, 3
289+  68AC 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
290+  68AF 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
291+  68B2 ED 5B D8 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
292+  68B6 B7                   or      a
293+  68B7 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
294+  68B9 22 E0 6A             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
295+  68BC 7D                   ld      a, l
296+  68BD CD 10 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
297+  68C0 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
298+  68C3 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
299+  68C5 CD F3 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
300+  68C8 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
301+  68CB
302+  68CB                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
303+  68CB 3A E1 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
304+  68CE CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
305+  68D1 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
306+  68D4 AF                   xor     a
307+  68D5 CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
308+  68D8 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
309+  68DB 3E 40                ld      a, #40
310+  68DD CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
311+  68E0 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
312+  68E3
313+  68E3                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
314+  68E3 3E 04                ld      a, 4
315+  68E5 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
316+  68E8 2A D6 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
317+  68EB ED 5B D8 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
318+  68EF              .bootstrap_block_loop:
319+  68EF 7A                   ld      a, d
320+  68F0 B3                   or      e
321+  68F1 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
322+  68F3 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
323+  68F4 CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
324+  68F7 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
325+  68FA 23                   inc     hl
326+  68FB 1B                   dec     de
327+  68FC 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
328+  68FE              .bootstrap_block_done:
329+  68FE
330+  68FE                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
331+  68FE                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
332+  68FE 3E 05                ld      a, 5
333+  6900 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
334+  6903 AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
335+  6904 CD 10 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
336+  6907 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
337+  690A 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
338+  690C CD F3 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
339+  690F DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
340+  6912 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
341+  6914 CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
342+  6917 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
343+  691A
344+  691A                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
345+  691A 3E 06                ld      a, 6
346+  691C 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
347+  691F CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
348+  6922 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
349+  6925 FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
350+  6927 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
351+  692A
352+  692A                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
353+  692A 3E 07                ld      a, 7
354+  692C 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
355+  692F 2A DC 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
356+  6932 7D                   ld      a, l
357+  6933 CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
358+  6936 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
359+  6939 7C                   ld      a, h
360+  693A CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
361+  693D DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
362+  6940 CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
363+  6943 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
364+  6946 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
365+  6948 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
366+  694B
367+  694B                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
368+  694B 3E 08                ld      a, 8
369+  694D 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
370+  6950 2A DA 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
371+  6953 ED 5B DC 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
372+  6957              .firmware_stream_loop:
373+  6957 7A                   ld      a, d
374+  6958 B3                   or      e
375+  6959 28 31                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
376+  695B
377+  695B                      ; Refill the same 256-byte host buffer only after its current bytes
378+  695B                      ; have all been consumed. DE (firmware bytes left) survives the read.
379+  695B ED 4B CB 6A          ld      bc, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
380+  695F 78                   ld      a, b
381+  6960 B1                   or      c
382+  6961 20 11                jr      nz, .firmware_buffer_ready
383+  6963 D5                   push    de
384+  6964 CD A0 67             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
385+  6967 D1                   pop     de
386+  6968 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
387+  696B 21 00 01             ld      hl, 256
388+  696E 22 CB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
389+  6971 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
390+  6974              .firmware_buffer_ready:
391+  6974 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
392+  6975 CD 2D 6A             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
393+  6978 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
394+  697B 23                   inc     hl
395+  697C 1B                   dec     de
396+  697D 22 DA 6A             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
397+  6980 2A CB 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
398+  6983 2B                   dec     hl
399+  6984 22 CB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
400+  6987 2A DA 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
401+  698A 18 CB                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
402+  698C
403+  698C              .firmware_stream_done:
404+  698C                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
405+  698C 3E 09                ld      a, 9
406+  698E 32 EC 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
407+  6991 CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
408+  6994 DA 9E 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
409+  6997 FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
410+  6999 C2 9E 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
411+  699C
412+  699C AF                   xor     a
413+  699D C9                   ret
414+  699E
415+  699E              .upload_failed:
416+  699E 37                   scf
417+  699F C9                   ret
418+  69A0
419+  69A0              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
420+  69A0              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
421+  69A0              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
422+  69A0              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
423+  69A0
424+  69A0              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
425+  69A0              gs_boot_probe_rom:
426+  69A0 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
427+  69A2 CD CE 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
428+  69A5 D8                   ret     c
429+  69A6 CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
430+  69A9 D8                   ret     c
431+  69AA 32 E2 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
432+  69AD CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
433+  69B0 D8                   ret     c
434+  69B1 32 E3 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
435+  69B4 CD 66 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
436+  69B7 D8                   ret     c
437+  69B8 32 E4 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
438+  69BB 3A E2 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_low)
439+  69BE 47                   ld      b, a
440+  69BF 3A E3 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid)
441+  69C2 B0                   or      b
442+  69C3 47                   ld      b, a
443+  69C4 3A E4 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
444+  69C7 B0                   or      b
445+  69C8 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
446+  69CA 37                   scf
447+  69CB C9                   ret
448+  69CC              .rom_probe_ok:
449+  69CC B7                   or      a
450+  69CD C9                   ret
451+  69CE
452+  69CE              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
453+  69CE              gs_boot_send_command_long:
454+  69CE 32 E5 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
455+  69D1 3A E5 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
456+  69D4 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
457+  69D6 D5                   push    de
458+  69D7 16 10                ld      d, 16
459+  69D9              .long_outer:
460+  69D9 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
461+  69DC              .long_inner:
462+  69DC DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
463+  69DE 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
464+  69E1 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
465+  69E3 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
466+  69E5 0B                   dec     bc
467+  69E6 78                   ld      a, b
468+  69E7 B1                   or      c
469+  69E8 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
470+  69EA 15                   dec     d
471+  69EB 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
472+  69ED D1                   pop     de
473+  69EE 37                   scf
474+  69EF C9                   ret
475+  69F0              .long_done:
476+  69F0 D1                   pop     de
477+  69F1 B7                   or      a
478+  69F2 C9                   ret
479+  69F3
480+  69F3              gs_boot_send_command:
481+  69F3 32 E5 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
482+  69F6 3A E5 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
483+  69F9 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
484+  69FB 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
485+  69FE              .command_wait:
486+  69FE DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
487+  6A00 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
488+  6A03 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
489+  6A05 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
490+  6A07 0B                   dec     bc
491+  6A08 78                   ld      a, b
492+  6A09 B1                   or      c
493+  6A0A 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
494+  6A0C 37                   scf
495+  6A0D C9                   ret
496+  6A0E              .command_done:
497+  6A0E B7                   or      a
498+  6A0F C9                   ret
499+  6A10
500+  6A10              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
501+  6A10              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
502+  6A10              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
503+  6A10 32 E6 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
504+  6A13 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
505+  6A16              .argument_wait_free:
506+  6A16 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
507+  6A18 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
508+  6A1B CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
509+  6A1D 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
510+  6A1F 0B                   dec     bc
511+  6A20 78                   ld      a, b
512+  6A21 B1                   or      c
513+  6A22 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
514+  6A24 37                   scf
515+  6A25 C9                   ret
516+  6A26              .argument_slot_free:
517+  6A26 3A E6 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
518+  6A29 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
519+  6A2B B7                   or      a
520+  6A2C C9                   ret
521+  6A2D
522+  6A2D              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
523+  6A2D 32 E6 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
524+  6A30 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
525+  6A33              .data_wait_free:
526+  6A33 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
527+  6A35 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
528+  6A38 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
529+  6A3A 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
530+  6A3C 0B                   dec     bc
531+  6A3D 78                   ld      a, b
532+  6A3E B1                   or      c
533+  6A3F 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
534+  6A41 37                   scf
535+  6A42 C9                   ret
536+  6A43              .data_slot_free:
537+  6A43 3A E6 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
538+  6A46 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
539+  6A48 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
540+  6A4B              .data_wait_consumed:
541+  6A4B DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
542+  6A4D 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
543+  6A50 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
544+  6A52 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
545+  6A54 0B                   dec     bc
546+  6A55 78                   ld      a, b
547+  6A56 B1                   or      c
548+  6A57 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
549+  6A59 37                   scf
550+  6A5A C9                   ret
551+  6A5B              .data_consumed:
552+  6A5B E5                   push    hl
553+  6A5C 2A E8 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
554+  6A5F 23                   inc     hl
555+  6A60 22 E8 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
556+  6A63 E1                   pop     hl
557+  6A64 B7                   or      a
558+  6A65 C9                   ret
559+  6A66
560+  6A66              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
561+  6A66 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
562+  6A69              .receive_wait:
563+  6A69 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
564+  6A6B 32 EB 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
565+  6A6E CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
566+  6A70 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
567+  6A72 0B                   dec     bc
568+  6A73 78                   ld      a, b
569+  6A74 B1                   or      c
570+  6A75 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
571+  6A77 37                   scf
572+  6A78 C9                   ret
573+  6A79              .receive_ready:
574+  6A79 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
575+  6A7B 32 E7 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
576+  6A7E B7                   or      a
577+  6A7F C9                   ret
578+  6A80
579+  6A80              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
580+  6A80              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
581+  6A80              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
582+  6A80              neogs_wait_command_done:
582+  6A80
583+  6A80 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
584+  6A83              .cmd_wait:
585+  6A83 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
586+  6A85 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
587+  6A87 28 07                jr      z, .cmd_done
588+  6A89 0B                   dec     bc
589+  6A8A 78                   ld      a, b
590+  6A8B B1                   or      c
591+  6A8C 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_wait
592+  6A8E 37                   scf
593+  6A8F C9                   ret
594+  6A90              .cmd_done:
595+  6A90 B7                   or      a
596+  6A91 C9                   ret
597+  6A92
598+  6A92              neogs_wait_command_done_long:
598+  6A92
599+  6A92 D5                   push    de
600+  6A93 16 10                ld      d, #10
601+  6A95              .cmd_long_outer:
602+  6A95 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
603+  6A98              .cmd_long_inner:
604+  6A98 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
605+  6A9A CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
606+  6A9C 28 0B                jr      z, .cmd_long_done
607+  6A9E 0B                   dec     bc
608+  6A9F 78                   ld      a, b
609+  6AA0 B1                   or      c
610+  6AA1 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_long_inner
611+  6AA3 15                   dec     d
612+  6AA4 20 EF                jr      nz, .cmd_long_outer
613+  6AA6 D1                   pop     de
614+  6AA7 37                   scf
615+  6AA8 C9                   ret
616+  6AA9              .cmd_long_done:
617+  6AA9 D1                   pop     de
618+  6AAA B7                   or      a
619+  6AAB C9                   ret
620+  6AAC
621+  6AAC              neogs_wait_data_byte:
621+  6AAC
622+  6AAC 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT
623+  6AAF              .data_wait:
624+  6AAF DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
625+  6AB1 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
626+  6AB3 20 07                jr      nz, .data_ready
627+  6AB5 0B                   dec     bc
628+  6AB6 78                   ld      a, b
629+  6AB7 B1                   or      c
630+  6AB8 20 F5                jr      nz, .data_wait
631+  6ABA 37                   scf
632+  6ABB C9                   ret
633+  6ABC              .data_ready:
634+  6ABC DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
635+  6ABE B7                   or      a
636+  6ABF C9                   ret
637+  6AC0
638+  6AC0              ; TR-DOS NeoGS package loader state and fixed 8-character filename.
639+  6AC0 6E 65 6F 70  neo_fw_filename:        db "neoprog "
639+  6AC4 72 6F 67 20
640+  6AC8 00 00        neo_fw_load_buffer:     dw 0
641+  6ACA 00           neo_fw_load_remaining:  db 0
642+  6ACB 00 00        neo_fw_buffer_remaining: dw 0
643+  6ACD 00           neo_fw_load_track:      db 0
644+  6ACE 00           neo_fw_load_sector:     db 0
645+  6ACF 00           neo_fw_file_error:      db 0
646+  6AD0 00 00        neo_fw_file_length:     dw 0
647+  6AD2
648+  6AD2              ; Stage 2 package parser and transfer state.
649+  6AD2 00 00        neo_fw_package_source:  dw 0
650+  6AD4 00 00        neo_fw_package_length:  dw 0
651+  6AD6 00 00        neo_fw_bootstrap_source: dw 0
652+  6AD8 00 00        neo_fw_bootstrap_length: dw 0
653+  6ADA 00 00        neo_fw_source:          dw 0
654+  6ADC 00 00        neo_fw_length:          dw 0
655+  6ADE 00 40        neo_fw_bootstrap_address: dw #4000
656+  6AE0 00 00        neo_fw_block_length:    dw 0
657+  6AE2 00           neo_fw_rom_ram_low:     db 0
658+  6AE3 00           neo_fw_rom_ram_mid:     db 0
659+  6AE4 00           neo_fw_rom_ram_high:    db 0
660+  6AE5 00           neo_fw_command_byte:    db 0
661+  6AE6 00           neo_fw_tx_byte:         db 0
662+  6AE7 00           neo_fw_last_rx_byte:    db 0
663+  6AE8 00 00        neo_fw_bytes_sent:      dw 0

265   6AED                      INCLUDE "x86_loader.asm"
# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6AED              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6AED              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6AED              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6AED              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6AED              ;
  6+  6AED              ; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
  7+  6AED              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6AED              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
  9+  6AED              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6AED              ;
 11+  6AED              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 12+  6AED              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 13+  6AED              ; =========================================================================
 14+  6AED
 15+  6AED              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 16+  6AED
 17+  6AED              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 18+  6AED              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 19+  6AED
 20+  6AED              EXECUTE_X86_FILE_STAGE:
 20+  6AED
 21+  6AED 22 49 79             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME), hl
 22+  6AF0 32 4B 79             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE), a
 23+  6AF3 ED 73 41 79          ld      (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP), sp

243+  6D1B             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
243+  6D1B E5          >                    PUSH    HL
243+  6D1C 21 8C 7B    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
243+  6D1F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
243+  6D22 E1          >                    POP     HL
243+  6D23             >                ENDIF
244+  6D23
245+  6D23                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
246+  6D23                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
247+  6D23                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
248+  6D23                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
249+  6D23                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
250+  6D23                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
251+  6D23                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
252+  6D23 ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
253+  6D27 78                   ld      a, b
254+  6D28 B1                   or      c
255+  6D29 CA 02 70             jp      z, .empty_file_error
256+  6D2C ED 43 4C 79          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
257+  6D30
258+  6D30                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
259+  6D30 78                   ld      a, b
260+  6D31 FE 2F                cp      #2F
261+  6D33 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
262+  6D35 C2 3B 70             jp      nz, .task_too_large
263+  6D38 79                   ld      a, c
264+  6D39 FE FF                cp      #FF
265+  6D3B D2 3B 70             jp      nc, .task_too_large
266+  6D3E              .file_length_ok:
267+  6D3E                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
268+  6D3E 2A 4C 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
269+  6D41 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
270+  6D44 19                   add     hl, de
271+  6D45 5C                   ld      e, h
272+  6D46 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
273+  6D49 B7                   or      a
274+  6D4A CA 02 70             jp      z, .empty_file_error
275+  6D4D BB                   cp      e
276+  6D4E C2 0E 70             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
277+  6D51 32 50 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
278+  6D54
279+  6D54 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
280+  6D57 FE 10                cp      16
281+  6D59 D2 0E 70             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
282+  6D5C 32 52 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
283+  6D5F 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
284+  6D62 32 51 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
285+  6D65 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
286+  6D68 22 4E 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
287+  6D6B
288+  6D6B              .read_sector_loop:
289+  6D6B                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
290+  6D6B 3A 51 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
291+  6D6E 57                   ld      d, a
292+  6D6F 3A 52 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
293+  6D72 5F                   ld      e, a
294+  6D73 2A 4E 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
295+  6D76 06 01                ld      b, 1
296+  6D78 0E 05                ld      c, 5
297+  6D7A AF                   xor     a
298+  6D7B 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
299+  6D7E 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
300+  6D81 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
301+  6D84                      ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
302+  6D84 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
303+  6D87 47                   ld      b, a
304+  6D88 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
305+  6D8B B0                   or      b
306+  6D8C C2 1A 70             jp      nz, .sector_read_error
307+  6D8F
308+  6D8F                      ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
309+  6D8F 2A 4E 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
310+  6D92 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
311+  6D95 19                   add     hl, de
312+  6D96 22 4E 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
313+  6D99
314+  6D99                      ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
315+  6D99 3A 52 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
316+  6D9C 3C                   inc     a
317+  6D9D FE 10                cp      16
318+  6D9F 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
319+  6DA1 AF                   xor     a
320+  6DA2 32 52 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
321+  6DA5 3A 51 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
322+  6DA8 3C                   inc     a
323+  6DA9 32 51 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
324+  6DAC 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
325+  6DAE
326+  6DAE              .sector_same_track:
327+  6DAE 32 52 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
328+  6DB1
329+  6DB1              .sector_advanced:
330+  6DB1 3A 50 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING)
331+  6DB4 3D                   dec     a
332+  6DB5 32 50 79             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
333+  6DB8 C2 6B 6D             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
334+  6DBB
335+  6DBB                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_buf_loaded
335+  6DBB             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
335+  6DBB E5          >                    PUSH    HL
335+  6DBC 21 B0 7B    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_buf_loaded
335+  6DBF CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
335+  6DC2 E1          >                    POP     HL
335+  6DC3             >                ENDIF
336+  6DC3
337+  6DC3                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
338+  6DC3                      ; Stage 5C: transfer actual task. The DMA frame contains a two-byte
339+  6DC3                      ; length prefix, therefore the total transfer must fit in $3000 bytes.
340+  6DC3                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
341+  6DC3 ED 4B 4C 79          ld      bc, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
342+  6DC7 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
343+  6DCA CD 70 70             call    neogs_send_dma_block
344+  6DCD DA 47 70             jp      c, .task_dma_error
345+  6DD0                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_task_dma_ok
345+  6DD0             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
345+  6DD0 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
345+  6DD1 21 ED 7B    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_task_dma_ok
345+  6DD4 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
345+  6DD7 E1          >                    POP     HL
345+  6DD8             >                ENDIF
346+  6DD8
347+  6DD8                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
348+  6DD8                      ; Stage 5D: execute and fetch framed result.
349+  6DD8                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
350+  6DD8 CD D7 70             call    neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result
351+  6DDB DA 53 70             jp      c, .result_timeout
352+  6DDE
353+  6DDE                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_x86_result_ready
353+  6DDE             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
353+  6DDE E5          >                    PUSH    HL
353+  6DDF 21 21 7C    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_x86_result_ready
353+  6DE2 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
353+  6DE5 E1          >                    POP     HL
353+  6DE6             >                ENDIF
354+  6DE6 3A 54 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
355+  6DE9 6F                   ld      l, a
356+  6DEA 26 00                ld      h, 0
357+  6DEC                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
357+  6DEC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
357+  6DEC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
357+  6DED E5          >                    PUSH    hl
357+  6DEE E1          >                    POP     HL
357+  6DEF CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
357+  6DF2 E1          >                    POP     HL
357+  6DF3             >                ENDIF
358+  6DF3                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
358+  6DF3             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
358+  6DF3 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
358+  6DF4 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
358+  6DF5 3E 20       >                    LD      A, 32
358+  6DF7 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
358+  6DFA ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
358+  6DFC C1          >                    POP     BC
358+  6DFD F1          >                    POP     AF
358+  6DFE             >                ENDIF
359+  6DFE 2A 55 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
360+  6E01                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
360+  6E01             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
360+  6E01 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
360+  6E02 E5          >                    PUSH    hl
360+  6E03 E1          >                    POP     HL
360+  6E04 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
360+  6E07 E1          >                    POP     HL
360+  6E08             >                ENDIF
361+  6E08                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
361+  6E08             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
361+  6E08 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
361+  6E09 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
361+  6E0A 3E 0D       >                    LD      A, 13
361+  6E0C 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
361+  6E0F ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
361+  6E11 C1          >                    POP     BC
361+  6E12 F1          >                    POP     AF
361+  6E13             >                ENDIF
362+  6E13
363+  6E13 3A 54 79             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
364+  6E16 B7                   or      a
365+  6E17 C2 5F 70             jp      nz, .interpreter_error
366+  6E1A
367+  6E1A 2A 55 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
368+  6E1D CD 69 88             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16
369+  6E20 3E 0D                ld      a, 13
370+  6E22 CD 76 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
371+  6E25 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
372+  6E27 CD 76 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
373+  6E2A B7                   or      a
374+  6E2B C3 6B 70             jp      .loader_restore_stack
375+  6E2E
376+  6E2E              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
377+  6E2E              ; Error exits. All return through the same original-stack restoration.
378+  6E2E              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
379+  6E2E              .diag_dma_error:
380+  6E2E                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
380+  6E2E             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
380+  6E2E E5          >                    PUSH    HL
380+  6E2F 21 F6 79    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
380+  6E32 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
380+  6E35 E1          >                    POP     HL
380+  6E36             >                ENDIF
381+  6E36 37                   scf

383+  6E3A

385+  6E3B 21 2B 7A    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail
385+  6E3E CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
385+  6E41 E1          >                    POP     HL
385+  6E42             >                ENDIF
386+  6E42 37                   scf

388+  6E46

390+  6E4A CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString

543+  706B              .loader_restore_stack:
544+  706B 2A 41 79             ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
545+  706E F9                   ld      sp, hl
546+  706F C9                   ret
547+  7070
548+  7070              ; =========================================================================
549+  7070              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
550+  7070              ; =========================================================================
551+  7070
552+  7070              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
553+  7070              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
554+  7070              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
555+  7070              ;
556+  7070              ; Protocol:
557+  7070              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
558+  7070              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
559+  7070              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
560+  7070              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
561+  7070              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
562+  7070              neogs_send_dma_block:
563+  7070 78                   ld      a, b
564+  7071 B1                   or      c
565+  7072 CA CA 70             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
566+  7075 78                   ld      a, b
567+  7076 FE 40                cp      #40
568+  7078 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
569+  707A C2 CA 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
570+  707D 79                   ld      a, c
571+  707E B7                   or      a
572+  707F C2 CA 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
573+  7082              .dma_length_ok:
574+  7082 22 43 79             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
575+  7085 ED 43 45 79          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
576+  7089
577+  7089                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
578+  7089 C5                   push    bc
579+  708A E1                   pop     hl
580+  708B 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
581+  708E 19                   add     hl, de
582+  708F 7C                   ld      a, h
583+  7090 FE 30                cp      #30
584+  7092 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
585+  7094 C2 CA 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
586+  7097 7D                   ld      a, l
587+  7098 B7                   or      a
588+  7099 C2 CA 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
589+  709C              .dma_total_length_ok:
590+  709C 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
591+  709E D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
592+  70A0 CD 80 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
593+  70A3 D8                   ret     c
594+  70A4
595+  70A4                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
596+  70A4                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
597+  70A4                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
598+  70A4                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
599+  70A4                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
600+  70A4 F3                   di
601+  70A5 2A 45 79             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
602+  70A8 22 47 79             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
603+  70AB 21 47 79             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
604+  70AE 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
605+  70B1 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
606+  70B4 ED B0                ldir
607+  70B6
608+  70B6 2A 43 79             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
609+  70B9 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
610+  70BC ED 4B 45 79          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
611+  70C0 ED B0                ldir
612+  70C2
613+  70C2                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
614+  70C2 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
615+  70C4 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
616+  70C6 CD 80 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
617+  70C9 C9                   ret
618+  70CA
619+  70CA              .dma_bad_length:
620+  70CA 37                   scf
621+  70CB C9                   ret
622+  70CC
623+  70CC              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
624+  70CC              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
625+  70CC              neogs_run_and_get_result:
626+  70CC 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
627+  70CE D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
628+  70D0 CD ED 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
629+  70D3 D8                   ret     c
630+  70D4 C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
631+  70D7
632+  70D7              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
633+  70D7              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
634+  70D7 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
635+  70D9 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
636+  70DB CD ED 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
637+  70DE D8                   ret     c
638+  70DF C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
639+  70E2
640+  70E2              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
641+  70E2              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
642+  70E2              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
643+  70E2 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
644+  70E4 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
645+  70E6 CD ED 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
646+  70E9 D8                   ret     c
647+  70EA C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
648+  70ED
649+  70ED              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
650+  70ED              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
651+  70ED              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
652+  70ED              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
653+  70ED D5                   push    de
654+  70EE 16 10                ld      d, #10
655+  70F0              .run_outer:
656+  70F0 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
657+  70F3              .run_inner:
658+  70F3 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
659+  70F5 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
660+  70F7 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
661+  70F9 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
662+  70FB B7                   or      a
663+  70FC 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
664+  70FE F5                   push    af
665+  70FF 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
666+  7102 ED 79                out     (c), a
667+  7104 F1                   pop     af
668+  7105 CD 76 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
669+  7108 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
670+  710A
671+  710A              .stream_control:
672+  710A CD AC 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
673+  710D 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
674+  710F B7                   or      a
675+  7110 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
676+  7112 FE 01                cp      1
677+  7114 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
678+  7116 CD AC 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
679+  7119 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
680+  711B 32 5A 79             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
681+  711E CD AC 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
682+  7121 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
683+  7123 4F                   ld      c, a
684+  7124 3A 5A 79             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
685+  7127 CD 68 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
686+  712A 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
687+  712C
688+  712C              .stream_clear:
689+  712C CD 1D 87             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
690+  712F 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
691+  7131
692+  7131              .no_stream_byte:
693+  7131 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
694+  7133 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
695+  7135 2B                   dec     hl
696+  7136 7C                   ld      a, h
697+  7137 B5                   or      l
698+  7138 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
699+  713A 15                   dec     d
700+  713B 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
701+  713D              .run_timeout:
702+  713D D1                   pop     de
703+  713E 37                   scf
704+  713F C9                   ret
705+  7140              .run_done:
706+  7140 D1                   pop     de
707+  7141 B7                   or      a
708+  7142 C9                   ret
709+  7143
710+  7143              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
711+  7143              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
712+  7143 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
713+  7145 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
714+  7147 CD 92 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
715+  714A D8                   ret     c
716+  714B C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
717+  714E
718+  714E              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
719+  714E              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
720+  714E 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
721+  7150 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a

723+  7155 D8                   ret     c
724+  7156 C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
725+  7159
726+  7159              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
727+  7159              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
728+  7159 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
729+  715B D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
730+  715D                      ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
731+  715D                      ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
732+  715D CD ED 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
733+  7160 D8                   ret     c
734+  7161 C3 6C 71             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
735+  7164
736+  7164              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
737+  7164              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:

741+  716B D8                   ret     c
742+  716C
743+  716C              neogs_get_result_frame:
744+  716C 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
745+  716E D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a

829+  7918              ; Run one diagnostic payload through DMA, execute it, and compare both
830+  7918              ; interpreter status and AX. IN: HL=payload, BC=length, A=expected status,
831+  7918              ; DE=expected AX. OUT: carry clear on exact match, set on any transport/result
832+  7918              ; mismatch. This deliberately accepts the expected error statuses above.
833+  7918              neogs_run_diag_case:

848+  7939 ED 52                sbc     hl, de

# file opened: bios_console.asm
  1+  8700              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8700              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
  3+  8700              ; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
  4+  8700              ; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
  5+  8700              ; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.
  6+  8700              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8700
  8+  8700              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 42
  9+  8700              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS    EQU 12
 10+  8700              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP     EQU 12
 11+  8700
 12+  8700              BIOS_DebugConsole_Init:
 12+  8700
 13+  8700 3A 2D 64             ld      a, (Console_Y)
 14+  8703 D6 0C                sub     BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 15+  8705 30 01                jr      nc, .row_ok
 16+  8707 AF                   xor     a
 17+  8708              .row_ok:
 18+  8708 FE 0C                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
 19+  870A 38 02                jr      c, .row_valid
 20+  870C 3E 0B                ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS - 1
 21+  870E              .row_valid:
 22+  870E 32 B8 88             ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
 23+  8711 3A 2C 64             ld      a, (Console_X)

  1+  8967              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8967              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8967              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8967              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8967              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
  6+  8967              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8967
  8+  8967              ; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
  9+  8967              ; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
 10+  8967              ;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
 11+  8967              GetSelectedFileDescriptor:
 11+  8967

 14+  896C 38 1C                jr      c, .bad_row

 16+  8970 30 18                jr      nc, .bad_row

 19+  8975 26 00                ld      h, 0
 20+  8977 29                   add     hl, hl
 21+  8978 29                   add     hl, hl
